5308 — ez bar russian twist sit up

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 5308 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supine.

A sit-up that adds a twist while holding an EZ bar. Works the abs and obliques together.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Lie on your back with knees bent and hold the EZ bar over your chest.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Lie on your back with knees bent and hold the EZ bar over your chest. 5308.json#/instructions/0
  2. Curl up into a sit-up position. 5308.json#/instructions/1
  3. At the top, rotate your torso to one side, moving the bar with you. 5308.json#/instructions/2
  4. Rotate back to center and lower yourself with control. 5308.json#/instructions/3
  5. Alternate sides each rep. 5308.json#/instructions/4
  6. Keep the weight light enough to move smoothly. 5308.json#/instructions/5

5309 — ez bar shoulder press sit up

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 5309 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supine.

Combine a sit-up with an overhead press using an EZ bar. Trains the abs, shoulders and triceps in one flowing rep.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Lie on your back with knees bent, holding the EZ bar at your chest. Sit up while keeping the bar close to your body.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 5309.json#/instructions/0, 5309.json#/instructions/1

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: EZ curl bar. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 5309.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Lie on your back with knees bent, holding the EZ bar at your chest. 5309.json#/instructions/0
  2. Sit up while keeping the bar close to your body. 5309.json#/instructions/1
  3. Once upright, press the bar overhead. 5309.json#/instructions/2
  4. Lower the bar back to your chest. 5309.json#/instructions/3
  5. Roll back down slowly to the floor. 5309.json#/instructions/4
  6. Brace your core and avoid yanking with your neck. 5309.json#/instructions/5

5310 — elevated side lunge

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 5310 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A side lunge performed with the lunging foot on a raised platform for extra range. Works glutes and inner thighs.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand tall next to a low step or platform.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 5310.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 5310.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Use a comfortable hip-width stance with soft knees unless the source specifies a single-leg, split or wider stance; keep the documented support foot grounded.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 5310.json#/instructions/0

For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 5310.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand tall next to a low step or platform. 5310.json#/instructions/0
  2. Step one foot sideways onto the platform. 5310.json#/instructions/1
  3. Push your hips back and bend that knee while the other leg stays straight. 5310.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep your chest up and the planted foot flat. 5310.json#/instructions/3
  5. Push back to the start. 5310.json#/instructions/4
  6. Complete your reps, then switch sides. 5310.json#/instructions/5

5311 — exercise ball serratus wall slide

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 5311 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: source_defined_support.

Slide your forearms up a wall on a stability ball to wake up the serratus anterior and improve shoulder control.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Place a stability ball against a wall and rest your forearms on it.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 5311.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Preserve any source-defined grip, crossed-arm position or moving-arm cue. Otherwise rest the arms clear of the moving segment in a comfortable balance position for owner review.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 5311.json#/instructions/0

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 5311.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Place a stability ball against a wall and rest your forearms on it. 5311.json#/instructions/0
  2. Step back slightly so your body leans in. 5311.json#/instructions/1
  3. Slide your forearms up the ball as you reach overhead. 5311.json#/instructions/2
  4. Push into the ball and spread your shoulder blades at the top. 5311.json#/instructions/3
  5. Slide back down slowly. 5311.json#/instructions/4
  6. Keep your ribs down and avoid arching your lower back. 5311.json#/instructions/5

5312 — rear lunge from deficit

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 5312 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A reverse lunge starting from a raised platform so you go deeper. Great for glutes and quads.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand on a low step with your feet hip-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 5312.json#/instructions/0

  6. Finish your reps, then switch legs. 5312.json#/instructions/5

5313 — dumbbell good morning squat

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 5313 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Blend a good morning hinge with a squat while holding a dumbbell. Builds the posterior chain and legs.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Hold a dumbbell at your chest and stand with feet shoulder-width apart.

Complete ordered source instructions

  4. Drive up through your heels. 5313.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat the hinge and squat as one rep. 5313.json#/instructions/4
  6. Keep your chest up throughout. 5313.json#/instructions/5

5315 — cable glute dominant step up

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 5315 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A step-up with a cable that is set to put more work on the glutes. It builds strength in the hips.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Hold the cable handle and stand facing a step. Place one foot on the step.

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supine.

Self-massage with a foam roller to ease tightness in the glutes. Good before training or on rest days to loosen up.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Lie on your back with the roller under one glute. Place the roller under the glutes and let your body weight settle onto it.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 5391.json#/instructions/0, 5391.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Rest the arms beside the torso with relaxed hands on the documented support surface; this is a balance/staging choice, not an added pressing action.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 5391.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

For an unspecified unloaded face-up setup, bend the knees comfortably and ground the soles; preserve any source-defined straight-leg or elevated-leg action.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 5391.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Foam massage roller. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 5391.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Lie on your back with the roller under one glute. 5391.json#/instructions/0
  2. Place the roller under the glutes and let your body weight settle onto it. 5391.json#/instructions/1
  3. Let the knees drop to one side and twist gently. 5391.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause on tender spots and breathe slowly for 20-30 seconds. 5391.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your core braced and avoid rolling over joints or bone. 5391.json#/instructions/4
  6. Roll for 30-60 seconds, then switch sides if needed. 5391.json#/instructions/5

5392 — roll hamstrings and glute sitting on floor

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 5392 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

Self-massage with a foam roller to ease tightness in the hamstrings and glutes. Good before training or on rest days to loosen up.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit with the roller under your thighs and hands behind you. Place the roller under the hamstrings and glutes and let your body weight settle onto it.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 5392.json#/instructions/0, 5392.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Sit with the roller under your thighs and hands behind you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 5392.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

For an otherwise unspecified seated setup, place the feet in a stable comfortable support position; preserve any source-defined footrests, pedals, leg pads or floor contact.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 5392.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Foam massage roller. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 5392.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit with the roller under your thighs and hands behind you. 5392.json#/instructions/0
  2. Place the roller under the hamstrings and glutes and let your body weight settle onto it. 5392.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lift your hips and roll from the knee toward the glutes. 5392.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause on tender spots and breathe slowly for 20-30 seconds. 5392.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your core braced and avoid rolling over joints or bone. 5392.json#/instructions/4
  6. Roll for 30-60 seconds, then switch sides if needed. 5392.json#/instructions/5

5394 — roll tibialis anterior

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 5394 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: quadruped.

Self-massage with a foam roller to ease tightness in the shin. Good before training or on rest days to loosen up.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Kneel on all fours with the roller under your shins. Place the roller under the shin and let your body weight settle onto it.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 5394.json#/instructions/0, 5394.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Place open palms on the support under the shoulders, with relaxed forward-facing fingers; this exact placement is an authored staging choice.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 5394.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Place the knees under the hips on the documented support and let the lower legs rest comfortably; preserve any source-defined lifted limb.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 5394.json#/instructions/0

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 5394.json#/instructions/0

  2. Place the roller under the shin and let your body weight settle onto it. 5394.json#/instructions/1
  3. Roll gently from below the knee to the ankle. 5394.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause on tender spots and breathe slowly for 20-30 seconds. 5394.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your core braced and avoid rolling over joints or bone. 5394.json#/instructions/4
  6. Roll for 30-60 seconds, then switch sides if needed. 5394.json#/instructions/5

5398 — roll foot

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 5398 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

Self-massage with a foam roller to ease tightness in the sole of the foot. Good before training or on rest days to loosen up.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand or sit with the roller under one foot. Place the roller under the sole of the foot and let your body weight settle onto it.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand or sit with the roller under one foot. 5398.json#/instructions/0
  2. Place the roller under the sole of the foot and let your body weight settle onto it. 5398.json#/instructions/1
  3. Roll the sole from heel to toes, then switch feet. 5398.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause on tender spots and breathe slowly for 20-30 seconds. 5398.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your core braced and avoid rolling over joints or bone. 5398.json#/instructions/4
  6. Roll for 30-60 seconds, then switch sides if needed. 5398.json#/instructions/5
